&lt;/project_context>
SSH 到 westd-29999 检查 14B eval batch 最终状态。

**验证服务器**: `nvidia-smi | head -5` 必须显示 4× ~97887 MiB。如果是 24GB 卡，你在错误服务器。

**背景**: 14B eval 的 bash 脚本 PID 93847 已退出，但 cuda:0 仍有 PID 99071 (32.7GB VRAM)。需要搞清楚：
- eval 是否已全部完成？
- PID 99071 是什么进程？

**检查步骤**:

1. `nvidia-smi | head -20` — 确认 westd + cuda:0 状态
2. `ps aux | grep 99071 | grep -v grep` — 查明 PID 99071 是什么
3. `tmux ls 2>&1` — 查看 eval_14b_batch session 是否还在
4. 检查 eval 结果（核心）：
   ```
   echo "=== eval_results_14b 目录 ==="
   ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/exp001/eval_results_14b/ 2>/dev/null
   
   echo "=== 已完成的 eval_results.json ==="
   ls /root/autodl-tmp/exp001/eval_results_14b/*/eval_results.json 2>/dev/null
   
   echo "=== 读取所有结果 ==="
   for f in /root/autodl-tmp/exp001/eval_results_14b/*/eval_results.json; do
     echo "=== $(dirname $f | xargs basename) ==="
     cat "$f"
     echo
   done
   ```
5. 如果结果不在 eval_results_14b 目录，检查其他可能位置：
   ```
   ls /root/autodl-tmp/exp001/eval_results/Qwen2.5-14B*/eval_results.json 2>/dev/null
   ls /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp001/eval_results*/Qwen2.5-14B*/eval_results.json 2>/dev/null
   ```

**输出**: 列出每个 14B adapter (d000-d100 s42) 的 MMLU accuracy 和 ARC accuracy。标记缺失的 adapter。报告 cuda:0 当前进程和是否可释放。

| d050_s42 | **IN PROGRESS** (PID 99071 on cuda:0) | **IN PROGRESS** |

The d050 evaluation just kicked off a few minutes ago and is actively running on cuda:0 with high GPU utilization, while d100 is queued and ready to go once that completes.

Looking at the GPU allocation, all four cards are occupied—cuda:1 and cuda:2 have other processes consuming significant VRAM, and cuda:3 is running the phi-4 training job. The batch script that launched these evals has already exited, which makes sense if it was designed to spawn each evaluation sequentially rather than wait for completion. The d050 eval is still going strong as its own independent process, and once it finishes, d100 will automatically start.

Actually, wait—if the wrapper script already exited, d100 won't launch automatically unless there's another process managing the queue. Let me check what's actually keeping d050 running and whether anything will trigger d100 next.
